# DTE Asymmetry Audit — Phase 0

Read-only inventory of 30 DTE / 15 DTE coverage gaps. Drives Phase 1 (drift-guard parity), Phase 2 (mechanism registry), Phase 3 (sweep harness).

Generated by `experiments/_dte_audit/audit.py`. Re-run any time.

---

## 1. Mechanism Coverage Matrix

For every field declared on `DteStrategyConfig` / `OptionStrategyConfig`, whether the four MC/BC engines define it as a module-level constant, and whether the drift-guard tracks it.

**Summary:**

- DteStrategyConfig fields: **127**
- OptionStrategyConfig fields: **18**
- monte_carlo.py module-level consts: 325
- monte_carlo_15dte.py module-level consts: 122
- backtest_cascade.py module-level consts: 206
- backtest_cascade_15dte.py module-level consts: 89
- drift-guard pairs_mc entries: 147
- drift-guard pairs_bc entries: 124
- drift-guard pairs_15 entries: 0

**Asymmetry counts (lower is better — gap fields are silent-error risks):**

- Fields wired in `monte_carlo.py` but NOT `monte_carlo_15dte.py`: **72** of 145
- Fields wired in `backtest_cascade.py` but NOT `backtest_cascade_15dte.py`: **74** of 145
- Fields drift-guarded for 30 DTE but NOT 15 DTE: **134** of 145

## 2. Experiment Script DTE Declarations

Every `experiments/**/*.py` that imports `monte_carlo`, `monte_carlo_15dte`, `backtest_cascade`, or `backtest_cascade_15dte`. Risk indicators: missing `DTE = N` declaration, alias collision (`mc` used for 15 DTE engine), missing holdout enforcement.

- Scripts importing an MC/BC engine: **182**
- Without `DTE = N` declaration: **180**
- 15 DTE engine aliased as `mc` (looks like 30 DTE at a glance): **3**
- Aliasing 30 DTE and 15 DTE engines with the same alias: **2**
- No holdout helper used (CALIBRATION_CUTOFF_DATE / assert_no_holdout_leak / pre_cutoff_filter): **175**

## 3. .cache/ Parquet Naming

Per `process.md` "On-demand bulk cache pattern", names should encode `(experiment, version, lookback)`. **DTE is not in the canonical convention** — this audit shows how many parquets currently encode it.

- Total parquets under `.cache/`: **7330**
- Filename encodes DTE (e.g. `_15dte_`, `_30dte_`): **0**
- Filename encodes version (`_vNN_`): **31**
- Filename encodes lookback (`_NNNN.parquet` or `_Ny.parquet`): **242**

## 4. Punch List → drives Phases 1–3

Concrete next steps, sized to the gaps surfaced above.

### 4.1 Drift-guard parity (Phase 1)

- Add **134** missing fields to `pairs_15` in `tests/test_strategy_config_drift.py` (mirror `pairs_mc` shape).
- Import `backtest_cascade_15dte` and add `pairs_bc15` array — currently 0 fields tracked there.
- Replace the SAW-style self-comparison `(s.X, s.X)` checks with real engine-vs-config asserts when the engine wires the constant.
- Add `check_dte_field_parity()` that walks `STRATEGY_30DTE.__dict__` vs `STRATEGY_15DTE.__dict__` — schema parity check, fails if a field exists on one but not the other.

### 4.2 Mechanism registry (Phase 2)

- Create `mechanism_registry.py` listing every mechanism. The 72 fields wired in `monte_carlo.py` but NOT `monte_carlo_15dte.py` are the immediate registry rows: each needs a `dte_15` status declaration with a `not_validated_reason`.
- Wire registry into drift-guard so "shipped to 30 DTE without explicit 15 DTE decision" fails CI.
- Auto-generate the `known-issues.md` CURRENT SHIP STATE table from registry.

### 4.3 Sweep harness hardening (Phase 3)

- Of 182 MC/BC-importing scripts, **180 lack `DTE = N` declaration**. Linter gate to require it.
- **3 scripts alias the 15 DTE engine as `mc`** — copy-paste hazard. Convention: `mc` for 30 DTE only, `mc15` for 15 DTE.
- **175 scripts** don't reference any holdout helper. Audit each: if it post-dates 2026-05-15, must gate.
- **7330 cache parquets** lack DTE in filename. If any are MC-output-derived (vs raw signal data), they need renaming or DTE-tagging.
